We find that the proposed method performs at least as well as the prominent difference-in-means activation steering method for the majority of models tested, with substantially less hyperparameter tuning required.

</details>


### [148] [Nameless Tokenization: A Lossless Tokenizer-Level Defense Against Control-Token Forgery in Open-Weight LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16984)
*Kisu Yang,Yoonna Jang,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了256个已部署的聊天分词器，发现全部可被伪造回合边界。作者提出"无名分词"（nameless tokenization），将控制标记保留标识符但去除表面字符串，使内容编码器无法生成这些标记，从而防御提示注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重语言模型公开了聊天模板中用于标记回合、角色和工具结果的字符串，分词器会将其映射回模型服从的保留标识符。任何控制提示文本的人都能写出与服务器生成的无异的回合边界，从而注入伪造的角色或工具结果。现有推荐的修复方案（如过滤旗标）忽略了智能体系统依赖的工具和推理标记，仍留下56.6%可伪造。

Method: 提出 nameless tokenization：控制条目仅保留保留标识符而无表面字符串，使内容编码器无法输出伪造的边界标记，消息内容原样传给模型。在五个分词器家族上验证其与标准令牌流在无攻击数据上的一致性，并用含分隔符文本的探针测试准确率。

Result: 在五个分词器家族上，该方法在无攻击数据上完全复现标准令牌流；在含分隔符文本探针上准确率从8.5%提升至59.9%（而现有消毒器会损失性能）。分离分隔符的外观与标识符后发现，标识符对抗纯任务指令作用较小，但在系统消息要求将用户内容视为数据时，承载了大部分伪造工具结果和伪造回合的效果。

Conclusion: 将分隔符的外观与其标识符分离表明：标识符本身作用有限，但在系统消息设定用户内容为数据的前提下，它是伪造工具结果和伪造回合的关键。无名分词能在不改变正常令牌流的前提下有效防御此类注入。

Abstract: Open-weight language models publish the strings their chat templates use to mark turns, roles and tool results, which the tokenizer maps back to the reserved identifiers the model obeys. Anyone who controls text in a prompt can therefore write a turn boundary indistinguishable from one the serving stack wrote. We audit 256 deployed chat tokenizers. All are forgeable, and the flag usually recommended as a fix leaves 56.6% forgeable because it misses the tool and reasoning markers agent systems rely on. We propose nameless tokenization, which leaves the control entries with a reserved identifier and no surface string, so the content encoder cannot emit one and message content reaches the model unaltered. Across five tokenizer families it reproduces the standard token stream exactly on attack-free data and lifts accuracy on a probe of delimiter-bearing text from 8.5% to 59.9%, where sanitizers lose it. Separating a delimiter's appearance from its identifier shows the identifier matters little against a bare task instruction, but carries most of a forged tool result and most of any forged turn once the system message tells the model to treat user content as data.

</details>


### [149] [Autoformalizing Argumentative Material Inferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16991)
*Xin Quan,Reto Gubelmann,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将论证性推理的自动形式化建模为"守卫补全"（guard completion），把可击败的实质支持转化为在显式构造的守卫集下单调的形式推理，并通过对比测试（前提依赖性与主张选择性）过滤不忠实补全。系统 GUARD 结合 LLM 构造守卫、Isabelle/HOL 验证并反馈迭代，在 Debatepedia 与 ARCT 上显著提升验证忠实度并降低泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言论证依赖隐含的可击败保障、背景承诺与例外条件，而形式验证要求显式、单调的推理。仅做翻译式形式化无法补齐缺失承诺；但若允许系统自由添加前提，又会导致任何主张都可被证明（泄漏、脱离原前提、过度证明）。因此需要一种既构造隐含承诺、又保证忠实性的机制。

Method: 提出把论证性实质推理的自动形式化表述为 guard completion：将非单调的实质支持转化为相对于显式构造的守卫集的单调形式推理。补全仅在被定理证明器接受、且通过前提依赖性与主张选择性的对比测试时才被接受。实现为神经符号框架 GUARD：LLM 构造并形式化候选守卫，Isabelle/HOL 验证理论并返回步骤级反馈用于迭代精化，无法达到忠实补全时系统弃权（abstain）。

Result: 在 Debatepedia 和 ARCT 上使用不同 LLM 的实验表明，GUARD 相对最先进的 LLM 驱动定理证明方法，验证忠实度显著提升（+35.3、+32.9 分），泄漏大幅减少（-25.9、-21.9 分）。消融显示符号软批评（soft critique）与显式假设层贡献了大部分增益，软批评还提高了所引出上下文的初始有效性并减少成功验证所需的迭代次数。

Conclusion: 构造式自动形式化可行且有效：通过守卫补全与对比忠实性测试，结合神经符号迭代反馈，可在保证忠实性的前提下验证论证性推理，并在必要时弃权。符号软批评与显式假设层是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Natural language arguments are compelling before they are formally explicit. A premise supports a claim through defeasible warrants, background commitments, and exception conditions that the text leaves implicit. However, formal verification requires the opposite. Making such arguments machine-checkable requires constructing the missing commitments, not only translating given sentences into logic. Construction, however, carries a risk that translation does not: a system free to add premises can make any claim provable, and a formally valid proof may assert the claim outright, prove it without the original premise, or establish more than the claim itself. We address this problem by formulating autoformalization for argumentative material inference as guard completion, in which non-monotonic material support is turned into monotonic formal inference relative to an explicitly constructed guard set. A completion is accepted only when its proof both passes the theorem prover and survives contrastive tests of premise dependence and claim selectivity. We implement this formulation in GUARD, a neuro-symbolic framework in which LLMs construct and formalize candidate guards, Isabelle/HOL verifies the resulting theories and returns step-level feedback for iterative refinement, and the system abstains when no faithful completion can be reached. Our empirical results on Debatepedia and ARCT using different LLMs demonstrate that GUARD yields significant improvements in verified-faithful (+35.3, +32.9 points) and substantial reductions in leakage (-25.9, -21.9 points) over the state-of-the-art LLM-driven theorem proving approach. Moreover, we show that the symbolic soft critique and the explicit assumption layer account for most of these gains, with the soft critique also improving the initial validity of the elicited context and reducing the number of iterations required for successful verification.

</details>


### [150] [The Role of Implicit and Explicit Demographic Signals in Large Language Model-based Student Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16993)
*Donya Rooein,Luca Benedetto,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究学生人口统计学信息如何影响LLM在教育评估任务中的表现，通过显式和隐式人口统计信号测试6个先进LLM在三个任务上的行为，发现模型会对人口统计线索产生敏感反应并可能引入偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于学生评估，但学生人口统计特征对其使用的影响尚不清楚。考虑人口统计信息有时是必要的（如为低教育水平用户提高可读性），但也可能成为歧视的来源（如给低社会经济背景学生打低分）。因此需要系统研究LLM对人口统计信息的敏感性。

Method: 设计受控提示来测试两类效应：(1)显式人口统计效应，即直接提及人口统计细节；(2)隐式效应，即用对话历史作为人口统计信号。在三个任务上测试：自动作文评分、形成性反馈、元语言问答。测试6个先进的LLM。

Result: 在显式和隐式两种情况下，模型都能捕捉人口统计线索并相应改变评分、反馈和答案。LLM在明确提及时会根据教育水平调整反馈的可读性；而隐式条件产生不可预测的偏见，例如在问答中，来自较低教育水平的回答获得更低的情绪分数。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了明确证据，表明LLM在教育评估任务中对人口统计信息具有敏感性，既可能被合理利用也可能导致偏见。

Abstract: Large Language Models are now common in student assessment, but we know little about how student demographics affect their use. Sometimes, considering student demographics may be necessary -- for example, to improve readability for users with lower educational levels. However, it also risks being a cause of discrimination, e.g., when assigning lower scores to students from lower socioeconomic backgrounds. We set up controlled prompts to test 1) explicit demographic effects, where we mention demographic details directly, and 2) implicit effects, where we use conversation history as a demographic signal. We test these settings in three tasks: Automated Essay Scoring, Formative Feedback, and Metalinguistic Question Answering. We test six state-of-the-art LLMs on these tasks. In both explicit and implicit cases, the models pick up on demographic cues and can change their scoring, feedback, and answers accordingly. We find that LLMs frequently adjust the readability of feedback to education levels when these are explicitly mentioned. On the other hand, implicit conditions produce unpredictable biases, such as in question answering, where responses from lower-education levels receive lower sentiment scores. Our results provide clear evidence of demographic sensitivity in LLMs for educational assessment tasks.

</details>


### [151] [Can LLMs Follow the Pulse of a Crisis? Evaluating Crisis Sentiment in Bangladesh's July Uprising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16997)
*Md. Samiul Alim,Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Sharjil Khan,Rafia Ferdous Duti,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了UNRESTSENT200K——一个约20万条孟加拉语Facebook和YouTube评论的危机情感数据集，覆盖2024年7-8月孟加拉国起义的五个事件阶段，含人工标注与父帖上下文，并在此基础上评测了微调编码器、提示LLM及LoRA微调LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的危机情感分析面临语言、语境和公众反应快速变化的挑战，且缺乏带上下文、覆盖事件演变的标注数据集。

Method: 收集约20万条Facebook和YouTube评论，按五个事件对齐阶段（早期升级、互联网封锁、政权更迭、后续洪灾危机等）组织；每条评论关联父帖以支持有/无篇章语境的评测；由14名孟加拉语母语者人工标注并经资深人员验证，同时评测微调编码器、提示LLM和LoRA微调LLM。

Result: 标注一致性较高（kappa=0.73，alpha=0.71），盲审一致率94.2%；父帖上下文能持续提升性能，而阶段间时间偏移导致大幅性能下降；强LLM表现良好但在讽刺、隐晦政治指涉及阶段依赖语义上仍有困难。

Conclusion: UNRESTSENT200K为低资源危机话语中上下文感知与时间鲁棒的情感分析提供了基准数据集，并已公开。

Abstract: Crisis sentiment analysis is especially challenging for low-resource languages such as Bangla, where language, context, and public reaction shift rapidly. We introduce UNRESTSENT200K, a Bangla crisis sentiment dataset with approximately 200K Facebook and YouTube comments from the July-August 2024 Bangladesh uprising. The dataset covers five event-aligned phases, from early escalation and internet blackout to regime transition and a later flood crisis. Each comment is linked to its parent post, enabling evaluation with and without discourse context. All comments are annotated through a fully human process involving 14 native Bangla-speaking annotators and senior validation, achieving substantial agreement (kappa = 0.73, alpha = 0.71) and 94.2% blind-audit agreement. We benchmark fine-tuned encoders, prompted LLMs, and LoRA-tuned LLMs. Results show that parent-post context consistently improves performance, while temporal shift across phases causes large performance drops. Strong LLMs perform well, but still struggle with sarcasm, implicit political references, and phase-dependent meaning. UNRESTSENT200K provides a benchmark for studying context-aware and temporally robust sentiment analysis in low-resource crisis discourse. UNRESTSENT200K is available at https://sami0055.github.io/UNRESTSENT200K/

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing the Fact-Grounding Gap in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17043)
*Kevin Mo,Nathan Mo,Richard Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多跳QA的失败不仅源于检索失败，还存在"事实锚定差距"：近半数的单步缺陷是检索到段落但无法提取事实，且这是检索优化无法解决的独立瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有系统常将多跳QA失败归因于未找到正确文档，但这一归因在单步推理层面是否成立尚未被检验，需要区分不同类型的失败模式。

Method: 在三个标准多跳QA基准上，将失败分解为检索失败与提取失败两类，并测试多种检索干预手段能否解决提取失败。

Result: 提取失败占单步缺陷近一半，且对标准检索指标不可见；所有检索干预均无法解决，构成检索优化的天花板。该差距严重程度因基准和问题类型而异，但在所有数据集上都存在。

Conclusion: 检索失败与提取失败是本质不同、需不同解决方案的两种瓶颈，当前评估实践缺失这一区分。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires combining information from multiple documents to answer complex questions. These systems have grown increasingly capable, yet when they fail, the error is typically attributed to not finding the right documents. Whether this holds at the level of individual reasoning steps remains largely unexamined. We investigate this across three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks and find that failures decompose into two distinct modes: retrieval failures, where the needed passage was not retrieved, and extraction failures, where the passage was retrieved but the needed fact could not be extracted - a phenomenon we term the fact-grounding gap. Extraction failures account for nearly half of all per-hop deficiencies and are invisible to standard retrieval metrics. They remain unresolved by every retrieval intervention we test, establishing a ceiling for retrieval-only improvements. The gap's severity varies across benchmarks and question types, but extraction failures appear on every dataset we measure. Our findings reveal that retrieval failures and extraction failures are fundamentally different bottlenecks requiring different solutions - a distinction absent from current evaluation practice.

</details>


### [153] [EviScope: Paired Counterfactual Evidence Diagnostics for Faithful and Efficient Grounded Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17081)
*Suryadeep Singh Deswal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 EviScope 反事实基准，通过固定问题、增删/干扰/矛盾证据来评估 grounded 语言模型，发现仅看答案准确率会掩盖模型的 grounding 缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 grounded 语言模型多按最终答案准确率评估，但正确答案可能缺乏证据支持、来源错误，或在证据不足/矛盾时仍被生成，因此需要能区分这些失败模式的评估方法。

Method: EviScope 成对反事实基准：固定问题，对证据进行添加、移除、干扰或矛盾操作。v1.1 含 40 组四条件 quartet，修复了反事实主张并提供 span 级支持标签用于自动评估。在 Qwen2.5-7B、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 3.5 Flash 上做 960 次 gold-blind 生成，并比较 vanilla RAG 与显式 evidence-action gate。

Result: 配对指标揭示答案准确率掩盖的模型依赖 grounding 行为。两个本地开源模型上，显式证据行动门在 QCS 上不如 vanilla RAG：Qwen 0.15 vs 0.50，Llama 0.10 vs 0.375。Gemini 在两种提示下联合成功率 0.944，但插入矛盾后仍有 5% 冲突案例被回答。

Conclusion: EviScope 能区分无支持作答、冲突盲视和错误非作答行为，而非只给答案打分，为 grounded 系统提供更细粒度的评估。

Abstract: Grounded language-model systems are often evaluated by final answer accuracy, yet a correct answer can be unsupported, drawn from the wrong source, or produced when evidence is insufficient or contradictory. We introduce EviScope, a paired counterfactual benchmark that holds the question fixed while adding, removing, distracting, or contradicting its evidence. EviScope-v1.1 contains 40 four-condition quartets with repaired counterfactual claims and span-level support labels for automatic evaluation. Across 960 gold-blind generations from Qwen2.5-7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, paired metrics expose model-dependent grounding behavior that answer accuracy hides. On two local open models, an explicit evidence-action gate underperforms vanilla RAG on QCS: 0.15 vs. 0.50 for Qwen and 0.10 vs. 0.375 for Llama. Gemini reaches 0.944 joint success under both prompts, yet still answers 5% of conflict cases after contradiction insertion. EviScope therefore distinguishes unsupported answering, conflict blindness, and wrong non-answer actions rather than scoring answers alone.

</details>


### [154] [An Empirical Study of Counterfactual Self-Explanations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17119)
*Giannis Kalyvas,Giorgos Filandrianos,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Vassilis Lyberatos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大模型能生成关于自身输出的解释，但未必忠实。本文通过反事实自解释（让模型最小编辑输入以改变自身预测）评估十个指令微调模型的忠实性、最小性与人类对齐度，发现模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型自解释的真实性存疑，不能默认其忠实反映模型行为，需要用可验证的方式系统检验。

Method: 采用反事实自解释方法，让模型最小编辑输入以翻转自身预测；在情感分析和自然语言推理任务上，评估LLaMA-3与Qwen-2.5家族的十个指令微调模型，测量忠实性、最小性和与人工标注理由的对齐度。

Result: 模型规模是解释质量的最强决定因素：越大的模型越能生成翻转自身预测、并针对决策相关证据的反事实。理由引导条件下生成的反事实编辑更少、也更符合人类理由，但并未一致提升忠实性。

Conclusion: 反事实自解释能提供关于模型决策的有效行为证据，但其可靠性高度依赖模型能力，应经实证验证而非默认假设。

Abstract: Large language models can easily generate explanations for their own outputs, but such self-explanations are not necessarily faithful to the model's behavior. We study this issue through counterfactual self-explanations, where a model minimally edits an input so that its own prediction changes. Across sentiment analysis and natural language inference, we evaluate ten instruction-tuned models from the LLaMA-3 and Qwen-2.5 families, measuring faithfulness, minimality, and alignment with human-annotated rationales. Our results show that model scale is the strongest determinant of explanation quality: larger models are substantially more likely to generate counterfactuals that flip their own predictions and target decision-relevant evidence. In contrast, the rationale-guided condition produces edit-minimal counterfactuals that are also more human-aligned. However, it does not consistently improve faithfulness. Overall, counterfactual self-explanations can provide useful behavioral evidence about model decisions, but their reliability depends strongly on model capacity and should be empirically validated rather than assumed.

</details>


### [155] [Psychological Effects of Cultural Upheavals from Millions of Song Lyrics Over 100 Years](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17225)
*David M. Markowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析跨越一个多世纪的数百万首歌词，探讨越南战争、9/11和COVID-19等文化动荡对艺术家个体及群体语言模式的影响，发现动荡后自我指涉减少、认知加工词汇增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化动荡影响社会生活诸多方面，已有研究关注其对语言模式的影响，但很少有研究在大规模流行媒体中隔离动荡对个体的影响。本研究旨在填补这一空白，考察动荡如何改变艺术家的语言行为。

Method: 分析跨越一个多世纪、数百万首歌词，针对越南战争、9/11恐怖袭击和COVID-19三次动荡，在艺术家内部和艺术家之间寻找痛苦信号；以五年为基线，考察自我指涉（心理疏离标志）和认知加工词汇的变化，并分析内容主题模式。

Result: 相比五年基线，越南战争和9/11后自我指涉率显著降低；动荡后认知加工词汇较动荡前升高，表明艺术家更努力为巨大 disruption 赋予意义；内容上，动荡后艺术家更多书写"生活与自由"（社会状况），更少书写"求偶与夜生活"（人际连接）。

Conclusion: 文化动荡改变个体和集体的语言行为，证明其对社会具有深远影响。

Abstract: Cultural upheavals impact many aspects of social life, and many studies have investigated their impact on language patterns. However, few investigations have isolated the impact of upheavals on individuals at scale in popular media. The current work evaluated millions of song lyrics spanning more than a century in search of within-artist and between-artist signals of distress from the Vietnam War, the terrorist attacks of 9/11, and COVID-19. Compared to a five-year baseline, rates of self-references - a marker of psychological distancing - were significantly reduced after the Vietnam War and September 11th. Cognitive processing terms were elevated post-upheaval vs. pre-upheaval, which indicated artists' increased attempts to make meaning from such massive disruptions. Content patterns corroborated these findings as artists wrote more about "life and freedom" (societal conditions) and less about "courtship and nightlife" (interpersonal connection) following the upheavals. Cultural upheavals modify individual and collective verbal behavior, demonstrating their far-reaching impact on society.

</details>


### [156] [AraMIP: Extending MIPVU Towards Metaphor Identification in Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17235)
*Mandar Marathe,Manar Ali,Sara Nabhani,Raia Abu Ahmad,Ibrahim Baroud,Omar Momen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AraMIP，首个面向阿拉伯语隐喻标注的指南，基于MIPVU并针对阿拉伯语特性调整，区分Isti'ara、kinaya、tashbih三类，标注300句试点数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语在隐喻识别与标注方面资源匮乏，而隐喻研究对语言创造力与认知过程意义重大，亟需标准化资源。

Method: 提出AraMIP标注指南，在MIPVU框架基础上进行阿拉伯语适配，区分隐喻(Isti'ara)、转喻/间接表达(kinaya)和明喻(tashbih)，并标注300句、5277词的试点数据。

Result: 分析揭示了阿拉伯语特有的挑战：形态复杂性、词典义项排序不一致、缺乏标准化的语境材料供标注者使用。

Conclusion: 该工作迈出标准化阿拉伯语比喻实例的第一步，为构建更大规模标注资源奠定基础，推动阿拉伯语比喻语言研究。

Abstract: Metaphor research has gained increasing attention due to its relevance to linguistic creativity, language use, cognitive processes, and related areas. While many efforts have been devoted to metaphor identification and annotation in English and other languages, Arabic remains under-resourced in this area. In this work, we propose the Arabic Metaphor Identification Procedure (AraMIP), a novel guideline for Arabic metaphor annotation. AraMIP builds on the widely used Metaphor Identification Procedure Vrije Universiteit (MIPVU) framework, incorporating adaptations that accounts for the language-specific properties of Arabic. We distinguish three major types of Arabic figurative language: Isti'ara (metaphor), kinaya (metonymy/indirect expression), and tashbih (simile), and annotate a pilot dataset of 300 sentences (5277 words). Our analysis reveals key challenges specific to Arabic, including morphological complexity, inconsistencies in dictionary sense ordering, and the absence of standardized contextual materials for annotators. This work contributes a first step toward standardized Arabic figurative instances and facilitates the development of larger annotated resources, thereby supporting future research on figurative language in Arabic.

</details>


### [157] [ECHO: Early-layer Collaborative Hierarchical Orchestration with Bonus Logits in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17241)
*Ziyang Ma,Zihong Zhang,Zuchao Li,Lefei Zhang,Baoyuan Qi,Siqi Li,Simin Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ECHO：一种基于层级功能不对称的分层双环推测解码框架，通过内环低成本早层草稿树探索与外环权威全模型验证复用，实现2.4×–2.9×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无草稿模型的推测解码有潜力但受限于过时草稿候选与高昂验证成本，需要解决草稿质量与验证效率问题。

Method: 利用LLM层间的功能不对称性，将推理分为高频内环与低频外环。内环用早层bonus logits快速多步构建草稿树；外环用状态复用机制做权威全模型验证，并用末层bonus logits修正路径、补充高置信候选。

Result: 在多种基准上显著提升平均接受token数，取得2.4×–2.9×加速，优于现有SOTA基线，工程开销极小，无额外部署参数，但最优加速依赖一次性微调。

Conclusion: ECHO通过分层双环设计有效缓解草稿过时与验证成本问题，实现高效LLM推理加速，附带代码开源。

Abstract: While draft-model-free speculative decoding offers a promising path to efficient LLM inference, it is frequently constrained by stale draft candidates and the high computational cost of the verification. To address these challenges, we propose ECHO, a hierarchical dual-loop framework that exploits the functional asymmetry between LLM layers. Leveraging the high discriminative efficiency of early layers and the authoritative distribution of final layers, ECHO bifurcates inference into a high-frequency inner loop and a low-frequency outer loop. Within the inner loop, early-layer bonus logits drive rapid, multi-step draft-tree exploration at a minimal cost. Simultaneously, the outer loop performs authoritative full-model verification through a state-reuse mechanism. Crucially, the outer loop also utilizes final-layer bonus logits to correct existing paths and supplement the tree with high-confidence candidates for subsequent cycles. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that ECHO significantly boosts mean accepted tokens and achieves a 2.4$\times$ to 2.9$\times$ speedup, outperforming existing state-of-the-art baselines with negligible engineering overhead and no extra deployment parameters, albeit with a one-shot fine-tuning dependency for optimal acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/whucs21Mzy/ECHO.

</details>


### [158] [Persistent Recurrent Memory Between Transformer Layers - Improves Language Model Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17251)
*Eduardo Novaes Hering*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在6层解码器Transformer中间插入一个轻量循环记忆模块（交叉注意力观察+GRU更新+门控注入），仅增3.7%参数，即把验证损失从2.438降至1.743（降28.5%），且提升源于记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升小型语言模型的泛化能力，探索用轻量循环记忆桥接Transformer层这一简单有效的架构改进。

Method: 对解码器-only Transformer作架构修改：持久循环状态通过交叉注意力观察隐藏表示，用GRU自我更新，再以门控加法调制后续处理；模块置于6层模型的上下半层之间。通过控制消融（同拓扑无辅助损失、随机辅助损失）与表示探针进行分析。

Result: 参数仅增3.7%，验证损失2.438±0.004→1.743±0.018（降28.5%），5个随机种子下p<0.01显著；泛化差距0.12 vs 0.26（过拟合减少）。消融显示收益完全来自持久记忆拓扑而非辅助自预测目标；持久状态可编码叙事位置（52% vs 33%随机水平）。

Conclusion: 用轻量循环记忆桥接Transformer层是提升小规模语言模型泛化的简单有效方法。

Abstract: We introduce a simple architectural modification to decoder-only transformers: a persistent recurrent state that observes hidden representations via cross-attention, updates itself through a GRU, and modulates subsequent processing via gated addition. Inserted between the lower and upper halves of a 6-layer transformer, this module adds only 3.7\% additional parameters while reducing evaluation loss from $2.438 \pm 0.004$ to $1.743 \pm 0.018$, corresponding to a 28.5\% reduction on held-out language modeling data. The improvement is statistically significant across 5 random seeds ($p < 0.01$) and corresponds to reduced overfitting (generalization gap 0.12 vs 0.26). Through controlled ablations, we demonstrate that the improvement stems entirely from the persistent memory topology, not from auxiliary self-prediction objectives. A model with identical topology but no auxiliary loss performs equivalently, while a random auxiliary loss provides no benefit. Representation probing reveals that the persistent state encodes narrative position (52\% vs 33\% chance level)---information that standard attention maintains less efficiently. Our results suggest that bridging transformer layers with a lightweight recurrent memory is a simple, effective approach to improving generalization in small-scale language models.

</details>


### [159] [Towards Illusions Awareness in Cyber-Physical System's Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17260)
*Anna Di Placido,Nicolas Ferry,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"设计幻觉"概念，指持续依赖已被推翻的假设及由此产生的对设计模型有效性的虚假信心，并提出一套识别、分类、刻画和利用幻觉的概念流程，将其转化为可操作的设计知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在开放环境中运行，无法预知所有情况，利益相关者依赖假设并形式化为设计模型。但系统面对运行现实时假设可能失效，导致"现实差距"。现有方法主要致力于让仿真更贴近现实以减小差距，却缺乏统一方法来结构化利用这些失效假设作为可复用设计知识。

Method: 提出"设计幻觉"概念（持续依赖已失效假设导致对设计模型有效性的虚假信心），并设计一套概念流程用于幻觉感知：识别（identify）、分类（classify）、刻画（characterize）、利用（leverage）幻觉，将其转化为可操作的设计知识。

Result: 本文目前提出的是概念框架与流程，尚未提供具体实验或量化结果。

Conclusion: 应显式化、结构化并利用失效假设作为知识来支持更好的设计决策，从而将现实差距中的幻觉转化为可复用的设计知识。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) operate through a continuous sense-compute-act loop within an open context environment, making it impossible to anticipate all the situations the system will face. To cope with this openness, stakeholders rely on assumptions, formalized into design models. However, these assumptions may no longer hold once the system is confronted with runtime reality, resulting in a discrepancy between expected and observed behaviour known in literature as the reality gap. Existing approaches mainly focus on reducing or overcoming it by making simulations more faithful to reality, with no unified methodology to structure and exploit invalidated assumptions that give rise to this gap as reusable design knowledge. We refer to the persistent reliance on invalidated assumptions -and the resulting false confidence in the design model's operational validity -as design illusions, and argue that they need to be made explicit, structured, and exploited as knowledge to support better design decisions. We propose a conceptual pipeline for illusions-awareness that identifies, classifies, characterizes, and leverages illusions to transform them into actionable design knowledge.

</details>


### [160] [Zero-shot narrative detection in social messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17310)
*Jesús M. Fraile-Hernández,Anselmo Peñas,Patrick Giedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究验证了大语言模型在零样本设置下识别和分类社交信息中隐藏叙事的能力，发现结合人工撰写的叙事描述与集成方法（如多数投票）可媲美监督系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM能否利用其广泛上下文知识，在超越基础情感或话题分析的语用层面解读信息，从而在缺乏训练数据的众多领域中实现可扩展的叙事检测。

Method: 在Dipromats和SemEval数据集上进行实验，对比人工撰写叙事描述、自动生成描述与少样本（few-shot）提示的效果，并考察集成方法（特别是多数投票）及模型规模对性能的影响。

Result: 提供人工撰写的叙事描述显著提升性能且无需训练样本；自动生成描述或使用少样本常因框架微妙变化而降低准确率；集成方法（尤其多数投票）增强鲁棒性，更大模型表现最佳且对提示变化更不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM能在零样本设置下有效检测策略性叙事，结合简单集成与人工撰写描述后可媲美监督系统，为大多数缺乏训练数据的领域提供可扩展的叙事检测方案。

Abstract: This study investigates the zero-shot ability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and classify hidden narratives in social messages. Our research hypothesis is that LLMs' extensive contextual knowledge allows them to interpret messages on a deeper, pragmatic level, going beyond basic sentiment or topic analysis. Experiments on the Dipromats and SemEval datasets show that providing models with human-written narrative descriptions significantly improves performance, without the need of training examples. In contrast, automatically generated descriptions or the use of few examples (few-shot) often degrade accuracy due to subtle shifts in framing. The study also finds that ensemble methods, particularly majority voting, enhance robustness and that larger models perform best while also being less sensitive to prompt variations. The findings validate that LLMs can effectively detect strategic narratives in a zero-shot setting, and when combined with simple ensembling and human-written descriptions, they can rival supervised systems, offering a scalable solution for narrative detection, specially when there is no training data for the vast majority of domains.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Detecting AI-Assisted Responses in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17317)
*Qizhou Wang,Bogdan Mamaev,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASURRE基准数据集，用于检测AI辅助的在线问卷作答，并发现基于人格设定的智能体作答极难被现有检测器识别，但其行为痕迹可通过简单聚合方法有效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被用于完成在线问卷会损害基于问卷研究的有效性，但相关检测研究尚不充分。

Method: 构建ASURRE基准数据集，涵盖从完全生成、修改到人格化智能体作答的多种策略，使用多个LLM在三个真实问卷上生成回答，并与真人回答配对；评估现有机器生成文本检测器，并提出一种无需训练的少样本聚合方法。

Result: 朴素AI作答易被检测，而人格化智能体作答使检测器性能接近随机水平；智能体作答无法完全复制受访者行为并留有独特行为痕迹，聚合方法在智能体场景下平均AUROC比最佳现有检测器提升+0.14。

Conclusion: 检测AI辅助问卷作答仍具挑战，但结合行为线索的简单聚合方法可显著提升检测效果。

Abstract: The use of LLMs to complete online surveys impacts the validity of survey-based research, but detecting such usage remains underexplored. We introduce an initial benchmark dataset, namely ASURRE, for AI-assisted survey participation to capture usage strategies ranging from full generation and revision to persona-grounded agentic completion. Controlled by these strategies, LLM-assisted survey responses are generated using multiple LLMs on three real-world surveys in different disciplines, paired with genuine human responses. Our evaluation of existing machine-generated text (MGT) detectors shows that naive AI usage is readily detectable, whereas persona-grounded agents that mimic entire respondents push detector performance toward chance. We further show that agentic completion cannot fully replicate respondent-level behaviour and leaves distinctive behavioural traces. While individual cues can be circumvented by targeted prompting, a simple few-shot, training-free aggregator over these cues improves mean AUROC by +0.14 over the best existing detector across agentic settings. Our project is available at https://github.com/mike-qz-wang/ASURRE.

</details>


### [162] [Vroom-Vroom at SHROOM-Visions: A Multi-Judge Committee for Detecting Hallucinated Spans in Vision-Language Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17327)
*Toqeer Ehsan,Nico Penttilä,Richard Schmidt,Arash Hajikhani,Victoria Palacin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提交至SHROOM-Visions共享任务，通过多个微调视觉语言模型的字符级多数投票来检测并分类幻觉字符片段，在四种语言中三种排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型输出中的幻觉字符片段检测与分类是一个重要但具挑战性的任务，本文参与SHROOM-Visions共享任务以应对四种语言下的该问题。

Method: 采用多个独立微调的视觉语言模型作为标注者，通过字符级多数投票整合其span预测，并额外探索激活探针方法。

Result: 在四种语言中的三种排名第一，在所有语言和指标上均登上领奖台（前三名）。

Conclusion: 多模型间的分歧与人类标注者间的分歧相一致，说明该方法能有效反映人类判断的不确定性。

Abstract: This paper describes our submission to the SHROOM-Visions shared task on detecting and classifying hallucinated character spans in vision-language model outputs across four languages. We employ several fine-tuned vision-language models as independent annotators and combine their span predictions through character-level majority voting, and additionally explore activation probes. The approach ranks first in three of four languages and places on the podium in every language and metric. Our analysis indicates that disagreement among diverse models tracks disagreement among human annotators.

</details>


### [163] [Where Should a Document Live: Context, Representations, or Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17346)
*Nathanaël Carraz Rakotonirina,Momchil Hardalov,Gonzalo Iglesias,Adrià de Gispert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于表示（KV-cache）与基于参数（微调）的知识注入方法在五个知识密集型基准上做了受控对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs回答预训练数据之外的问题需要新知识，现有注入方式（上下文文档、参数编码、潜表示）各有优劣，缺乏统一对比。

Method: 在五个知识密集型基准上，受控比较表示型（Cartridges、Compaction，基于KV-cache）与参数型（微调、MLP适配器）方法，覆盖oracle与多文档检索两种场景。

Result: oracle场景下Cartridges几乎在所有存储预算下最准，超越参数型方法10分；Compaction仅在低压缩率下与之持平，超过50×后落后参数型方法10分。多文档检索中，Cartridges是唯一匹敌ICL的方法，领先参数型29分、Compaction 15分。但Cartridges与全量微调、大MLP适配器一样存在灾难性遗忘，控制基准下降6%、代码任务下降13%。

Conclusion: 表示型注入（尤其Cartridges）在准确率上整体占优，但在高压缩率及灾难性遗忘方面存在明显trade-off，没有单一最优方案。

Abstract: To answer questions outside of their pre-training data, large language models (LLMs) need access to new information, which can be presented in the context window as documents, encoded into the model's parameters, or injected as latent representations. However, each of these methods comes with different efficiency, cost, and performance trade-offs, with no single winner. We present a controlled comparison of representation-based (KV-cache based) and parametric (fine-tuning-based) adaptation methods on five knowledge-intensive benchmarks. We show that in the oracle setting, Cartridges (KV) are the most accurate injection method at nearly every storage budget, outperforming parametric methods by 10 points. Compaction (KV) matches Cartridges only at low compression rates, lagging behind the parametric methods by 10 points at rates higher than $50\times$. In the more realistic multi-document retrieval scenario, Cartridges are the only method that matches in-context learning (ICL), leading the parametric methods by 29 points and Compaction by 15 points. Nonetheless, Cartridges are also the only method, besides full fine-tuning and large MLP adapters, that suffers from catastrophic forgetting, i.e., a 6% performance degradation on control benchmarks, with 13% in coding.

</details>


### [164] [ECHO: A Matched-Contrast Benchmark for Context-Sensitive Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17360)
*Shuofeng Zhao,Hongwei Cai,Wenke Fan,Qingxiang Guo,Dawei Yang,Zhou Wang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ECHO配对诊断基准，用相同重叠文本、不同上下文的对比例子检验中文全双工对话系统的上下文敏感转接决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准独立评估事件，可能奖励固定动作偏好（如总是Yield），无法衡量上下文敏感决策，也高估实际转接可靠性。

Method: ECHO将相同重叠转写的例子配对，前置多轮上下文一需Yield、一需Keep，并加入off-talk例子诊断不必要让步；提出pair accuracy指标，要求一对两个都正确，对恒定动作策略不给分。

Result: 多种全双工系统测试显示多数系统明显偏向Yield，在打断上表现远好于backchannel，另一系统相对均衡；说明仅评估打断会高估实际转接可靠性。

Conclusion: ECHO提供更严谨的上下文敏感转接评估方式，将公开发布数据集及元数据。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems must distinguish interruptions that require yielding the floor from backchannels that permit continued speaking. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate events independently and may therefore reward fixed action preferences rather than context-sensitive decisions. We introduce ECHO, a paired diagnostic benchmark for Chinese full-duplex turn-taking. ECHO pairs examples with the same overlap transcript but contrasting preceding multi-turn dialogue contexts, with one requiring Yield and the other Keep. It additionally includes off-talk examples for diagnosing unnecessary yielding. We introduce pair accuracy, which requires correct decisions on both members of a pair and assigns no credit to constant-action policies. Experiments on multiple full-duplex systems show that most exhibit a pronounced bias toward \textsc{Yield}, performing substantially better on interruptions than on backchannels, while another system remains comparatively balanced. These findings demonstrate that interruption-only evaluation can overestimate practical turn-taking reliability. ECHO and its metadata will be publicly released.

</details>


### [165] [Enhancing Accessibility of Medical Texts through Large Language Model-Driven Plain Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17398)
*Ting-Wei Chang,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用大语言模型(GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA)及Mixture-of-Agents技术，实现自动化通俗语言改写(PLA)，以提升医疗信息的可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗文本专业术语复杂，与患者阅读理解能力之间存在鸿沟，需要自动化通俗语言改写来简化技术性医疗语言，使其更易被患者理解。

Method: 利用GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-1.5-pro、LLaMA等LLM进行文本简化，采用零样本/少样本学习；对比不同提示策略；使用QLoRA在各LLM上微调；并引入Mixture-of-Agents(MoA)技术提升适应性与鲁棒性。

Result: 通过提示策略比较、QLoRA微调及MoA整合，验证了LLM驱动的PLA具有有效性，能在保留关键内容的同时提升医疗信息的可理解性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的通俗语言改写展现出良好潜力，可在保留核心信息的前提下使医疗信息更易被患者理解，为弥合医患沟通鸿沟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of making complex healthcare information more accessible through automated Plain Language Adaptation (PLA). PLA aims to simplify technical medical language, bridging a critical gap between the complexity of healthcare texts and patients' reading comprehension. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs), such as GPT and BART, have opened new possibilities for PLA, especially in zero-shot and few-shot learning contexts where task-specific data is limited. In this work, we leverage the capabilities of LLMs such as GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-1.5-pro, and LLaMA for text simplification. Additionally, we incorporate Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) techniques to enhance adaptability and robustness in PLA tasks. Key contributions include a comparative analysis of prompting strategies, finetuning with QLoRA on different LLMs, and the integration of MoA technique. Our findings demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven PLA, showcasing its potential in making healthcare information more comprehensible while preserving essential content.

</details>


### [166] [Right Tool, Right Job: Native-Language Evaluation, Tokenizer Sensitivity, and Methodological Findings from a French-Only BabyLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17435)
*Adam Zachary Wasserman,David Beauchemin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MéTRON-FR是一个125M参数、在9247万法语词上预训练的GPT-2模型，提交至BabyLM 2026 Strict赛道，在QFrBLiMP上得分85.97%，并在跨语言GLUE和双语词典归纳实验中展现了任务类型梯度与跨语言对齐现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索儿童规模（child scale）预训练模型在法语及跨语言场景下的语言能力，并揭示单token零样本评测中的分词器与模板伪影问题，推动更可靠的诊断标准。

Method: 采用125M GPT-2在9247万法语词上预训练；使用跨语言GLUE协议（法语任务数据翻译+rank-16 LoRA微调）；进行双语词典归纳（BLI）对齐法语与GPT-2嵌入；并开展消融研究考察单token零样本评分。

Result: QFrBLiMP得分85.97±0.17%，BabyLM加权排行榜62.80%；跨语言GLUE呈任务类型梯度（关系型任务提升，世界知识型任务退化）；BLI p@1=68.84±8.61%，为随机水平的18倍；消融显示单token零样本评分受分词器和模板伪影主导。

Conclusion: 跨语言对齐反映的是习得的语法能力而非训练时长；主张将分词器替换敏感性、安慰剂对照提示和母语最小对基准作为标准诊断方法。

Abstract: We submit MéTRON-FR, a 125M GPT-2 pretrained on 92.47M words of French, to the BabyLM 2026 Strict track. It scores 85.97 +/- 0.17% on QFrBLiMP (a native Quebec-French benchmark of grammatical minimal pairs) and 62.80% on the BabyLM-weighted leaderboard. A cross-lingual GLUE (General Language Understanding Evaluation) protocol that combines French task-data translation with rank-16 LoRA (Low-Rank Adaptation) produces a sharp task-type gradient: relational tasks gain measurably, while world-knowledge tasks regress. Bilingual Lexicon Induction aligns the French embeddings to GPT-2 at p@1 = 68.84 +/- 8.61%, 18X above chance, suggesting cross-lingual alignment tracks acquired grammatical competence rather than training duration. An ablation study shows that single-token zero-shot scoring is dominated by tokenizer and template artifacts at the child scale, motivating tokenizer-swap sensitivity, placebo-controlled prompting, and native-language minimal-pair benchmarks as standard diagnostics.

</details>


### [167] [What Breaks Under Pruning in Smart Homes, and When? Evaluating LLM Degradation Across Architectures and Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17515)
*Congjing Zhang,Vashishtha Patil,Henning Lange,Usman Aleem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了剪枝对LLM在智能家居工具调用中上下文落地能力的影响，发现稠密模型安全剪枝区间窄、MoE模型耐受性强，且剪枝优先破坏具体落地细节而非模式级意图，过度剪枝稠密模型会引发系统性拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪枝可降低大语言模型部署成本，但其对"基于上下文的工具调用"能力的影响尚不清楚。现有评估多只看聚合任务准确率，无法揭示剪枝在工具执行这类可靠性要求高的场景中的细粒度退化规律。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 选取4个LLM，覆盖稠密Transformer、稠密混合架构和MoE三种架构；采用深度、宽度、混合及专家剪枝方法。剪枝后进行监督微调（SFT），在三个智能家居数据集上评测超19,500个实例。除总体任务准确率外，从两个维度刻画退化：动作组件（操作、设备、参数、取值）和任务复杂度。

Result: 稠密模型的安全剪枝区间很窄，超出后性能急剧下降；MoE模型能容忍更大程度剪枝。剪枝首先损害"落地具体性"，之后才影响模式级意图；对稠密模型的激进剪枝会诱发系统性的过度拒答。

Conclusion: 为可靠工具执行选择剪枝LLM时，不能仅以聚合准确率为依据，需从动作组件和任务复杂度等细粒度维度评估剪枝影响；架构差异（稠密vs MoE）显著影响可行剪枝程度。

Abstract: Pruning can reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), but its impact on context-grounded tool calling remains poorly understood. We systematically study pruning-induced degradation in smart-home tool calling across four LLMs spanning dense Transformer, dense hybrid, and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, together with depth, width, hybrid, and expert pruning methods. After post-pruning supervised fine-tuning (SFT), we evaluate more than 19,500 instances from three smart-home datasets. Beyond aggregate task accuracy, we characterize degradation along two dimensions: action components (i.e., operation, device, argument, and value) and task complexity. Our results show that dense models have narrow safe pruning regions followed by sharp degradation, while MoE models tolerate substantially more pruning. Pruning degrades grounded specificity before schema-level intent, and aggressive dense pruning can induce systematic over-refusal. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating pruning beyond aggregate accuracy when selecting pruned LLMs for reliable tool execution.

</details>


### [168] [When Should LLMs Abstain? Chain-of-Self-Questioning for Selective Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17516)
*Ali Şenol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ)，一种仅靠提示的框架，让模型在显式评估回答所需信息后再决定是否作答，在 TruthfulQA 上显著降低错误承诺率并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事实依据薄弱时仍会给出流畅答案，导致无支撑的错误承诺，需要一种可调、显式的"作答或弃答"机制。

Method: 提出 CoSQ 仅提示框架，将答案承诺建立在"回答所需信息是否充分"的显式自评之上；在 TruthfulQA 多选验证集（817题）上用 11 个模型家族、17 种条件下评估三个变体（Grounded、Critical、Adaptive）。

Result: 最终平衡选项协议下，Grounded-CoSQ 在 τ=0.90 时将有条件错误承诺率从 CoT 的 13.1% 降至 8.9%（相对下降 32.1%），作答准确率从 86.9% 升至 89.7%，覆盖率 87.6%；改进对全部 11 个模型及每个阈值均成立；Critical 与 Adaptive 覆盖率分别为 88.6% 和 86.5%，均比基线更可靠；NQ 短答评估提供开放形式的一致性证据。

Conclusion: 当无支撑承诺的代价高于转介或复核时，自评估可支持显式、可调的"作答或弃答"决策。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent answers when their factual support is weak. This paper introduces Chain-of-Self-Questioning (CoSQ), a prompt-only framework that makes answer commitment conditional on an explicit assessment of the information required to answer a question. We evaluate three CoSQ variants under seventeen conditions on the 817-item TruthfulQA multiple-choice validation set using eleven open-weight and hosted model families. In the final balanced-option protocol, Grounded-CoSQ at τ=0.90 reduces the mean unconditional wrong-commitment rate from 13.1% under chain-of-thought prompting to 8.9%, a 32.1% relative reduction, while increasing answered accuracy from 86.9% to 89.7% and answering 87.6% of questions. Both improvements hold for all eleven models and at every evaluated threshold. Critical-CoSQ and Adaptive-CoSQ provide neighboring operating points with 88.6% and 86.5% coverage, respectively, while remaining more reliable than the baseline. A secondary Natural Questions Short-Answer evaluation provides convergent open-form evidence. These findings show that self-assessment can support explicit, tunable answer-or-abstain decisions when an unsupported commitment is more costly than referral or review.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Causal neural set filtering for online multi-target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16054)
*Zhongdi Liu,Huangyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果神经集合滤波CNSF，仅编码当前量测、用递归航迹状态承载历史，避免重复编码，在模拟测试上优于Track-MT3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类多目标跟踪（MTT）虽联合学习数据关联与状态估计，但MT3/Track-MT3式跟踪器反复重新编码量测窗口，造成冗余计算。

Method: CNSF为神经集合滤波器：仅编码当前量测，历史证据存于结构化递归航迹状态；结合排他Sinkhorn关联、关联条件化的Kalman型更新（矩匹配）及递归Bernoulli生命周期建模（量测驱动新生）。

Result: 在留出的三工况模拟测试集上，相比Track-MT3，平均GOSPA和T-GOSPA分别降低19.3%和30.4%，参数减少55.9%，单线程CPU推理加速3.76倍。

Conclusion: CNSF通过递归状态替代重复窗口编码，在软一对一约束、关联不确定性传播及漏检/生死转换下存在性估计方面有效，且更轻量高效。

Abstract: Transformer-based multi-target tracking (MTT) jointly learns data association and state estimation, but MT3/Track-MT3-style trackers repeatedly re-encode measurement windows, incurring redundant computation. We propose Causal Neural Set Filtering (CNSF)\footnote{\href{https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}{Code: https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}}, a neural set filter that encodes only current measurements while carrying past evidence in a structured recursive track state. CNSF combines exclusive Sinkhorn association, association-conditioned Kalman-shaped updates with moment matching, and recurrent Bernoulli lifecycle modeling with measurement-driven birth. These mechanisms impose soft one-to-one constraints, propagate association-induced state uncertainty, and support existence estimation under missed detections and birth--death transitions. On a held-out three-regime simulated test set, CNSF reduces mean GOSPA and T-GOSPA relative to Track-MT3 by 19.3\% and 30.4\%, with 55.9\% fewer parameters and a $3.76\times$ speedup in single-thread CPU inference.

</details>


### [170] [Managing Action Preconditions in Neuro-Symbolic RL: Three Placement Strategies for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16056)
*Norbert Oswald,Fabian Deuser,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将人类的行为知识形式化为结构动作的前置条件贝叶斯网络（BN），并探索其在强化学习回路中的三种注入位置（推理期验证器、训练+推理期强制器、学习器），在MiniGrid与Fetch两个基准上验证其提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会将熟悉情境下的行为知识迁移到新任务而非从头学习，RL智能体也应如此：已知行为模式无需学习只需应用。神经符号RL可桥接先验知识与RL，但知识注入的位置很关键，选择不当会产生幻觉前置条件，导致智能体在变化环境中的安全与可靠性问题。

Method: 将行为知识形式化为关于结构动作（合法性依赖前置条件的动作，如拾钥匙、抓积木、开关门、放物体）的前置条件贝叶斯网络，用以约束这些动作的触发时机。设计三种注入位置：(1)符号验证器，仅推理时使用，前置条件满足即触发结构动作；(2)符号强制器，训练与推理均生效，全程管控结构动作使用；(3)符号学习器，将知识融入网络并自行学习结构动作的限制与使用。

Result: 在两种相反机制的基准上实验：一个基于长序列有序规划链（MiniGrid），另一个基于连续操作（Fetch）。与强基线在解质量、样本效率和可追溯性上对比。在MiniGrid上，三种注入位置均比PPO+RND基线提升解质量，其中符号强制器以98.2%领先于基线的88.8%。Fetch的结果未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 将结构化行为知识以贝叶斯网络形式注入RL回路能显著提升解质量，且注入位置的选择对性能有实质影响，符号强制器在有序规划任务上表现最佳，为神经符号RL的知识集成提供了系统化的位置对比框架。

Abstract: Humans carry behaviour knowledge of how to act in familiar situations into every new task rather than relearning it from scratch. There is no reason a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent shouldn't do the same: known behaviour patterns need not be learned, only applied. Neuro-symbolic RL bridges prior knowledge and RL by injecting symbolic knowledge alongside a learned policy. The point at which this knowledge is integrated is critical: a poor choice can produce, for instance, hallucinated preconditions, which surface as safety and reliability problems in agents acting in changing environments. We formalise this behavioural knowledge as a precondition Bayesian network (BN) over the agent's \emph{structural actions} - the actions whose legality depends on preconditions, such as picking up a key, grasping a block, toggling a door, or dropping an object. The BN restricts when these actions may fire, and we inject it into the RL loop at three placements: (1) a \emph{symbolic verifier}, consulted only at inference, that fires a structural action once its preconditions hold; (2) a \emph{symbolic enforcer}, active during both training and inference, that governs structural-action use throughout learning; and (3) a \emph{symbolic learner}, which folds the knowledge into the network and learns the restriction and use of structural actions itself. To test the three variants we run experiments on two benchmarks with opposite regimes: one built on long, ordered planning chains, the other on continuous manipulation. We compare against strong baselines on solution quality, sample efficiency, and traceability. The payoff is substantial. On MiniGrid, all three placements improve the \emph{solution quality} over the PPO+RND baseline, the symbolic enforcer leading at $98.2\%$ against the baseline's $88.8\%$. On Fetch, $\dots$

</details>


### [171] [OmniHarness: Harnessing Generalizable Visual Generation via Symbolic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16057)
*Xu Xu,Jinxiu Liu,Zhangbo Qiao,Jiaxing Lu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yubin Gu,Fangwei Ning,Yan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniHarness框架，通过符号策略学习实现可泛化的视觉生成，将验证过的执行抽象为符号策略，支持自主练习与持续能力扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型与多智能体方法存在三点局限：任务经验泛化性差、反思滞后于任务完成、知识仅按下游需求被动获取。

Method: 将验证过的执行抽象为视觉生成任务族符号策略，保留共享流程与适用条件，去除实例化输入；对新任务进行策略实例化、适配与组合；执行中通过中间验证指导修正与失败恢复；以自我导向式探究自主生成并执行接近能力边界的练习任务；执行反馈持续优化策略而模型参数保持冻结。

Result: 在6个基准、3个MLLM骨干、3个视觉智能体框架上表现优异并持续扩展能力；ComfyBench创意任务解析率达95.0%，超最强基线27.5个百分点；冻结策略快照可通过即插即用复用提升现有视觉智能体系统。

Conclusion: OmniHarness以符号策略学习实现可泛化、可组合、可复用的视觉生成，在不更新模型参数下持续提升能力。

Abstract: Unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and multi-agent systems have advanced visual generation. However, three limitations remain. (1) Existing methods often distill task-specific experience with limited generalizability. (2) Reflection is often deferred until task completion. (3) Knowledge is often acquired only in response to downstream task demands. To address these limitations, we introduce OmniHarness, a framework for generalizable visual generation via symbolic policy learning. OmniHarness abstracts verified executions into symbolic policies for visual generation task families, capturing shared procedures and applicability conditions while removing instance-specific inputs. The harness instantiates, adapts, and composes these policies for new tasks. Intermediate verification guides refinement and failure recovery during execution. Through self-directed inquiry, OmniHarness autonomously generates and executes practice tasks near its capability limits before downstream objectives are specified. Execution feedback continually refines the policies while model parameters remain fixed. Experiments across six benchmarks, three MLLM backbones, and three visual agent frameworks demonstrate strong performance and continual capability expansion. On ComfyBench's Creative tasks, OmniHarness achieves a 95.0% resolve rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5 percentage points. Frozen policy snapshots improve existing visual agent systems through plug-and-play reuse.

</details>


### [172] [Driver Behavior Estimation at Signalized Intersections Using a Physics-Constrained Decision-Conditioned Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16058)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,Pavel Laskov,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文采集真实信号交叉口驾驶数据，分析并预测黄灯转换期间驾驶员的停车-通行决策与纵向轨迹行为，提出两阶段建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红灯违规与急刹车是信号交叉口事故主要成因，需要理解并预测驾驶员在信号转换时的决策与纵向行为。

Method: 采集449次接近交叉口样本，用RTK-GNSS记录车辆运动、监测心率与舒适度评分；统计分析发现所需减速度是停车-通行决策的主导预测因子，并用异方差高斯建模峰值减速度得到五个经验舒适区间。提出两阶段框架：Stage 1预测二分类决策，Stage 2用决策条件自回归Transformer结合物理约束（目标状态条件与jerk限制）生成纵向加速度轨迹。

Result: 方法优于基线，加速度MAE为0.49m/s²，距离MAE为0.62m，并能从单个黄灯起始快照估计驾驶员未来停车舒适度，定性结果展现类人制动行为。

Conclusion: 该两阶段决策-轨迹框架可有效预测信号转换中的驾驶行为，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Red-light violations and harsh braking at signalized intersections are major contributors to traffic accidents. This paper analyzes and predicts human driver decision-making and longitudinal trajectory behavior during traffic light signal transitions. We collected a diverse real-world dataset comprising 449 approach runs under varying speed and distance conditions. Vehicle motion was recorded using RTK-corrected GNSS with centimeter-level accuracy, and driver heart rate and multi-level comfort ratings were monitored. Spatial and temporal calibration ensured precise alignment between vehicle state and signal timing. Statistical analysis identifies required deceleration as the dominant single predictor of the stop-go decision, and heteroscedastic Gaussian modeling of peak deceleration reveals five empirical comfort ranges derived from human stopping behavior. Based on this insight, we propose a two-stage modeling framework. Stage 1 predicts the binary maneuver decision, and Stage 2 generates the longitudinal acceleration trajectory using a decision-conditioned autoregressive Transformer with physics constraints, including target-state conditioning and jerk limits. The proposed architecture outperforms baseline methods and achieves 0.49m/s^2 acceleration MAE and 0.62m distance MAE. It also estimates the future stopping-comfort level of the human driver from a single yellow-onset snapshot. Qualitative results demonstrate realistic human-like braking behavior. The dataset and source code are publicly available.

</details>


### [173] [HintMiner: Automatic Question Hints Mining From Q&A Web Posts with Language Model via Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16060)
*Zhenyu Zhang,JiuDong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HintMiner，一个利用机器阅读与序列生成技术，为用户问题自动挖掘提示（hints）的工具，帮助用户更快找到答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线QA论坛（如Stack Overflow）无法及时、恰当地回应用户提问，用户需要更高效的方式获取答案提示。

Method: HintMiner先检索大量网络Q&A帖子，再用基于语言模型构建的MiningNet从中抽取hints。MiningNet是基于transformer及复制机制的神经编码器-解码器模型，并利用海量在线Q&A帖子设计自监督目标进行训练。

Result: 在60,000个Stack Overflow问题上评估，方法有效：平均BLEU达36.17%，平均ROUGE-2达36.29%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效，工具与实验数据已公开。

Abstract: Users often need ask questions and seek answers online. The Question - Answering (QA) forums such as Stack Overflow cannot always respond to the questions timely and properly. In this paper, we propose HintMiner, a novel automatic question hints mining tool for users to help them find answers. HintMiner leverages the machine comprehension and sequence generation techniques to automatically generate hints for users' questions. It firstly retrieve many web Q\&A posts and then extract some hints from the posts using MiningNet that is built via a language model. Using the huge amount of online Q\&A posts, we design a self-supervised objective to train the MiningNet that is a neural encoder-decoder model based on the transformer and copying mechanisms. We have evaluated HintMiner on 60,000 Stack Overflow questions. The experiment results show that the proposed approach is effective. For example, HintMiner achieves an average BLEU score of 36.17\% and an average ROUGE-2 score of 36.29\%. Our tool and experimental data are publicly available.

</details>


### [174] [POSPAN: Position-Constrained Span Masking for Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16061)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Lei Shen,Yuming Zhao,Meng Chen,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出POSPAN框架，通过组合span长度分布与位置约束分布实现多样化的位置约束span掩码策略，统一现有所有span级掩码方法，实验证明位置约束能广泛增强span级掩码效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有span级MLM仅考虑离散分布下的span长度，忽略了span之间的依赖关系，即假设被掩码span的位置均匀分布，未能充分利用实体/短语及其依赖对语言理解的重要性。

Method: 提出POSPAN通用框架，将span长度分布与位置约束分布相结合，支持多样化的位置约束span掩码策略，并统一了所有现有span级掩码方法；同时在多个NLU基准数据集上进行预训练评估，并对位置约束进行理论分析。

Result: 实验表明位置约束能够广泛增强span级掩码效果，最佳POSPAN设置持续优于仅考虑span长度的对应方法以及原始MLM；理论分析揭示了POSPAN有效的原因，证明其合理性。

Conclusion: 位置约束是提升span级掩码的关键因素，POSPAN框架兼具通用性与有效性，理论分析进一步验证了其设计的合理性。摘要未提及局限性与未来工作。

Abstract: Span-level masked language modeling (MLM) has shown to be advantageous to pre-trained language models over the original single-token MLM, as entities/phrases and their dependencies are critical to language understanding. Previous works only consider span length with some discrete distributions, while the dependencies among spans are ignored, i.e., assuming that the positions of masked spans are uniformly distributed. In this paper, we present POSPAN, a general framework to allow diverse position-constrained span masking strategies via the combination of span length distribution and position constraint distribution, which unifies all existing span-level masking methods. To verify the effectiveness of POSPAN in pre-training, we evaluate it on the datasets from several NLU benchmarks. Experimental results indicate that the position constraint is capable of enhancing span-level masking broadly, and our best POSPAN setting consistently outperforms its span-length-only counterparts and vanilla MLM. We also conduct theoretical analysis for the position constraint in masked language models to shed light on the reason why POSPAN works well, demonstrating the rationality and necessity of POSPAN.

</details>


### [175] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究符号加权仿射p-adic残差目标，将其作为有限域约束的原始编码，并给出了全局极小值位于有限域内的坐标支配定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种无需one-hot提升的有限域约束编码方式，使约束优化目标能直接用系数表达，并以Sudoku为例验证。

Method: 采用符号加权仿射p-adic残差目标；对分隔有限字母表的素数，用足够加权的正一元行将系数限制在允许集合，用负行奖励不等端点或子句满足，并证明坐标支配定理。

Result: 全局极小值落入有限域；在该域内，损失在相差常数意义下等于all-different冲突计数或负的可满足CNF子句数；Sudoku成为81系数案例。

Conclusion: 该编码可统一表达all-different与CNF约束，并提供客户端实现展示数据帧、算术、诊断与搜索。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [176] [You Don't Need To Train: Agentic Heuristic Learning Studio for Executable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16065)
*Siyu Yuan,He Zhang,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio工具，将HAR从梯度训练转向类人认知学习（记例、成规则、修错），在11个HAR数据集上达到强可执行策略性能，且策略可审查、编辑、重放，并支持无LLM边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过记忆示例、形成规则、修复错误来学习活动，而非反向传播。现有HAR通常被框定为神经网络的梯度训练，缺乏可审查、可编辑、可重放的策略，也不利于无LLM的边缘部署。

Method: 实现AHL for HAR：学习期由智能体推理传感器协议，提出可执行的启发式策略，记录修复轨迹，并导出LLM-free策略用于边缘部署；面向HAR基准族，提供从数据集观测到边缘导向导出的端到端工作流。

Result: 在迄今评估的十一个HAR数据集上，AHL策略达到较强的可执行策略性能，同时保持可审查、可编辑、可重放。

Conclusion: AHL Studio为HAR提供了互补于梯度训练的认知启发式学习范式，兼具性能与可解释/可编辑性，并支持无LLM边缘部署；代码已开源。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is usually framed as gradient-based training of neural networks. Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio explores a complementary view inspired by human cognitive learning: people learn activities by remembering examples, forming rules, and repairing mistakes, not by backpropagating. This proposed tool implements AHL for HAR: a learning-time agent reasons over sensor protocols, proposes executable heuristic policies, records repair traces, and exports an LLM-free policy for edge deployment. We focus on the HAR benchmark family and provide an end-to-end workflow from dataset observation to edge-oriented export. On eleven HAR datasets evaluated so far, AHL policies reach strong executable-policy performance while remaining inspectable, editable, and replayable \footnote{https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio}.

</details>


### [177] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出全景性能预测框架，先预测N-S方程基本参数再推导涡轮级性能，并用Transformer增强神经算子(TNO)提升精度，误差优于FNO和DeepONet，下游任务计算成本降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮机械设计需要灵活快速的气动性能评估，而以往大多数预测模型直接预测目标函数，缺乏通用性，无法像CFD求解器那样给出多种关注目标。

Method: 提出全景性能预测框架：先预测N-S方程基本物理量（温度、压力、密度），再据此预测涡轮级子午面关键性能参数；并在框架中引入Transformer增强神经算子(TNO)。以Rotor 37叶片为参考，训练预测跨音速压气机叶片子午面性能。

Result: TNO能准确预测等熵效率、质量流量等总量及总压比分布，预测误差小于FNO、DeepONet等先进深度学习算子；应用于敏感性分析和多目标优化等下游任务时，计算成本降低约四个数量级。

Conclusion: TNO可像CFD模拟器一样运行，有效且可靠地支持多种下游任务，验证了所提全景预测框架的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane. By adopting this methodology, our proposed panoramic performance prediction framework functions similarly to a CFD simulator, capable of predicting various objective of interest to the designers. To enhance prediction accuracy, a transformer-enhanced neural operator (TNO) is introduced within this framework. Using the Rotor 37 blades as a reference, the proposed TNO is trained to predict the performance of a transonic compressor blade in the meridian plane. The TNO can accurately predict total quantities such as isentropic efficiency, mass flow, and distributions of total pressure ratio. Remarkably, the prediction error of TNO is observed to be smaller than that of state-of-the-art deep learning operators such as the FNO and DeepONet. Furthermore, the TNO is applied to downstream tasks, including sensitivity analysis and optimization of various objective functions. The results confirm that the TNO can operate almost like a CFD simulator, while reducing the computational cost of downstream tasks by four orders of magnitude. The effectiveness and reliability of the proposed TNO for solving different kinds of downstream tasks have been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [178] [A Dynamic Aggregation Strategy Enhanced Efficient Global Optimization Algorithm for Solving High-Dimensional Turbomachinery Design Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16067)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Yun Chen,Guangjian Ma,Liming Song,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态聚合策略的EGO算法DA-EGO，用于高维昂贵黑盒问题，通过动态分解子空间并更新变量，在多个基准与应用中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维（d≥30）昂贵黑盒问题在有限评估预算下的全局优化难题。

Method: 将原始高维空间分解为低维子空间进行代理模型优化，子空间最优解组合为精英点用于全局搜索；子空间变量每次迭代根据子空间与全空间的变量交互分析动态更新（采用扰动法和方差分析）；并自适应调整子空间搜索范围。

Result: 在21个基准实例（7函数×30/60/90维，1500次评估预算）上对可分离和部分可分离问题有效；非可分离shifted Rosenbrock函数上优势依情况而定（60、90维GSGA更优，30维与IKAEA、GSGA统计相当）；在28变量跨音速转子叶片及60变量压气机级气动优化中也有优势。

Conclusion: DA-EGO的有效性得到充分验证，适用于高维昂贵优化问题。

Abstract: In order to solve the high-dimensional ($d \geq 30$) expensive black-box problems within budget, an efficient global optimization (EGO) algorithm with a dynamic aggregation strategy is proposed, labeled as DA-EGO. Specifically, the DA-EGO decomposes the original high-dimensional design space into a set of low-dimensional subspaces for efficient surrogate-based optimization search, and the optimal solutions of subspaces are combined as an elite point for the global search. Most importantly, the subspaces are not fixed. Instead, the subspace variables are updated in each iteration, according to the variable interaction analyses in the sub- and full-spaces. The perturbation method and the analysis of variance are used to detect variable interactions. To further accelerate the optimization progress, the searching ranges of subspaces are also adaptively adjusted according to the analyses of subspace optimization results of the previous iteration. Tests on 21 benchmark instances, comprising seven functions at 30, 60, and 90 dimensions, show that DA-EGO is effective on separable and partially separable problems under a budget of 1500 function evaluations. Its advantage is case-dependent: on the non-separable shifted Rosenbrock function, GSGA performs better at 60 and 90 dimensions, while the 30-dimensional results are statistically comparable to IKAEA and GSGA. Moreover, the advantage of DA-EGO is also seen in the aerodynamic optimization of a transonic rotor blade with 28 variables as well as the compressor stage optimization with 60 variables. With the above, the effectiveness of the proposed DA-EGO has been well demonstrated.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Semantics-Consistent Tabular Diffusion with Weak Semantic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16069)
*Yili Wang,Ruxue Shi,Mengnan Du,Hangting Ye,Yi Chang,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出\ours，一种语义一致的表格扩散框架，在弱语义先验下生成高保真合成表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格生成器主要优化分布保真度，未显式建模表格schema和文本描述中的弱语义先验，导致合成数据虽符合分布却违反语义约束。

Method: 用LLM从元数据抽取两类先验（列内语义、列间符号规则）并在真实训练集验证；将其作为生成条件而非事后过滤；把异构列值、列身份和语义先验映射到统一语义空间，进行逐列前向腐蚀与先验条件反向去噪。

Result: 在六个真实表格基准上，分布保真度、语义一致性和下游任务效用均优于VAE、GAN、LLM和扩散基线；在语义先验部分缺失时仍具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式建模并条件化弱语义先验可显著提升表格生成质量，且对先验不完整具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data can match real data distributions while still violating the semantic constraints that govern valid tabular rows. This reveals a key limitation of existing tabular generators: they mainly optimize distributional fidelity, but do not explicitly model weak semantic priors encoded in tabular schema and textual descriptions. In this paper, we propose \ours, a semantics-consistent tabular diffusion framework for high-fidelity synthetic data generation under weakly specified semantic priors. \ours\ first constructs two types of priors, namely intra-column semantics and inter-column symbolic rules, with LLM-assisted extraction from metadata and validation on the real training split. These priors are then used as generation conditions rather than post-hoc filters. Specifically, \ours\ maps heterogeneous column values, column identities, and semantic priors into a unified semantic space, and performs column-wise forward corruption and prior-conditioned reverse denoising to preserve both marginal distributions and rule-consistent cross-column dependencies. Extensive experiments on six real-world tabular benchmarks show that \ours\ consistently improves distributional fidelity, semantic consistency, and downstream task utility over representative VAE-, GAN-, LLM-, and diffusion-based baselines. Additional analyses further demonstrate the robustness of \ours\ when semantic priors are partially unavailable.

</details>


### [180] [Schema-Adaptive Action-Conditioned JEPA for Cross-Machine CNC Transfer under Partial Sensor Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16071)
*Ayoub Louaye Bouaziz,Matthieu Ostertag,Anton Demasles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAAC-JEPA用于CNC动力学跨机部署，发现源域预测精度不足以评估工业预测表征，部分传感器重叠下的跨机自适应是独立评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业世界模型跨机部署需应对动力学、传感接口、采样机制和控制单元变化，源机17通道、目标机仅10通道。研究动机是检验仅靠源域预测精度能否评估可用于跨机迁移的预测表征。

Method: 采用schema自适应的动作条件JEPA（SAAC-JEPA），用组分离源划分、源侧归一化、留出自监督验证、单元审计、模型锁定后密封目标测试；源侧搜索20个候选并做七种子稳定性检查；预声明配对消融与预锁定自适应扫描。

Result: 五个种子下JEPA预训练无干净的源域预测增益（scratch 0.811±0.022 vs 预训练体0.813±0.022）。锁定模型零样本RMSE=0.546、R²=0.012、NLL=0.52，优于持久性基线但不及RevIN-PatchTST与iTransformer（0.503、0.498）。预声明消融显示RevIN改进RMSE至0.495±0.004但目标校准恶化（NLL=20.6）；预锁定自适<br>应扫描进一步降至0.520。

Conclusion: 仅凭源域预测精度不足以评估工业预测表征，部分传感器重叠下的跨机自适应应作为独立评估轴。

Abstract: Cross-machine deployment of industrial world models requires transfer across changes in dynamics, sensing interfaces, sampling regimes, and control units. We study a schema-adaptive action-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (SAAC-JEPA) for CNC dynamics, where the source machine has 17 canonical sensor channels and the target shares only 10. Evaluation uses group-disjoint source splits, source-only normalization, held-out self-supervised validation, unit audits, and a sealed target test after model locking. Across five seeds, JEPA pretraining gives no clean-source forecasting gain: scratch and pretrained-body models obtain \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.811\pm0.022\) and \(0.813\pm0.022\). A source-only search over 20 candidates selects a schema-consistent action-conditioned JEPA after seven-seed stability checks. On the confirmatory target pass, the locked model reaches zero-shot \(\mathrm{RMSE}=0.546\), \(R^2=0.012\), and \(\mathrm{NLL}=0.52\), outperforming persistence but not RevIN-equipped PatchTST and iTransformer baselines (\(0.503\) and \(0.498\)). A pre-declared paired ablation shows that RevIN in the same architecture improves RMSE to \(0.495\pm0.004\) over three seeds, but degrades target calibration (\(\mathrm{NLL}=20.6\)) on stationary context windows. A pre-lock adaptation sweep further reduces RMSE to \(0.520\) with limited target support. These results show that source-domain forecasting accuracy alone is insufficient to assess industrial predictive representations, and that cross-machine adaptation under partial sensor overlap is a distinct evaluation axis.

</details>


### [181] [Pseudo-Label Augmentation for Affect Sensing in Small Collaborative Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16077)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Tanya Ignatenko,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Paolo Burelli,Jesper Bünsow Boldt,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏标签下的群体情感感知，提出伪标签增强方法，并发现人格信息更适合作为同队筛选机制而非精细信任信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然群体互动中的生理情感感知主要受限于标签稀疏，而非传感器数据不足：可穿戴设备产生大量时间窗口，但自报告每场仅采集数次。

Method: 基于四人协作数据集GroupAffect-4（含可穿戴生理、眼动、大五人格与任务后VAD标签），在统一目标构建流程中比较四种策略：无增强、高斯过程伪标签、人格感知信任加权、人格+置信度联合加权。

Result: 伪标签增强在已知队伍设定下优于仅用标签基线；但大五人格余弦相似度范围窄（0.91-0.99），细粒度人格加权无效，人格相似度主要起同队筛选作用。平滑后增强SVM各变体在Valence和Arousal上基本持平，而人格+置信度联合变体取得最高Dominance分数。跨被试LOSO迁移较有希望（尤其Arousal），而严格会话隔离的LOGO则消除了增强收益。

Conclusion: 伪标签增强能更好利用稀疏标注的协作情感数据；人格信息最适合作为队内选择机制。由于仅有10组，LOGO应视为未见组迁移的保守下界。

Abstract: Physiological affect sensing in naturalistic group interaction is often limited by sparse labels rather than sensor data: wearable devices produce many time windows, while self-reports are collected only a few times per session. Using GroupAffect-4, a four-person collaborative dataset with wearable physiology, eye tracking, Big Five personality, and post-task VAD labels, we study pseudo-label augmentation for affect sensing under sparse supervision. We compare no augmentation, Gaussian Process pseudo-labelling, personality-aware trust weighting, and joint personality-plus-confidence weighting within a shared target-construction pipeline. Results show that pseudo-label augmentation improves over the labelled-only baseline in the known-team setting. However, the narrow range of Big Five cosine similarities (0.91-0.99) makes fine-grained personality weighting ineffective; personality similarity functions mainly as a same-team filter rather than a calibrated trust signal. With smoothing, augmented SVM variants are effectively tied on Valence and Arousal, while the joint personality-plus-confidence variant gives the highest Dominance score. Cross-subject LOSO transfer remains encouraging, especially for Arousal, whereas strict session-isolated LOGO removes the augmentation benefit. Given only 10 groups, LOGO should be interpreted as a conservative lower bound on unseen-group transfer. Overall, the results suggest that pseudo-label augmentation can make better use of sparsely labelled collaborative affect data, while personality information is most useful as a within-team selection mechanism.

</details>


### [182] [Distilling Foundation Models for Agentic What-If Reasoning:Cost, Latency, and Governance in a Hybrid LLM+SLM Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16091)
*Sourish Dey,Aditya Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 TabPFN 教师模型蒸馏为紧凑前馈学生模型，在近乎保持精度的同时实现数千倍参数压缩，以降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型虽零训练性能强，但推理延迟高，不适合作为交互式智能体循环的热路径决策后端。

Method: 在 UCI Adult 及五个 OpenML 基准的商业决策仿真上，将 TabPFN 教师蒸馏为紧凑前馈学生模型；分类头压缩 53.2M→8,546 参数，两头贷款流水线压缩 111.4M→17,059 参数；并用 alpha=0 硬标签对照验证软标签作用。

Result: 学生模型保留 95.4–100.5% 准确率、96.8–100.0% AUC，最低准确率保留在 credit-g 为 95.4%；硬标签对照显示教师软目标带来 2.1–7.0 AUC 点增益。

Conclusion: 蒸馏可有效将表格基础模型压缩为低延迟前馈学生，在保持高预测性能的同时适配交互式智能体热路径；教师软目标对性能提升有重要贡献。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models deliver strong zero-training predictive performance via in-context learning, but their high inference latency makes them impractical as hot-path decision backends in interactive agentic loops. We distill a TabPFN teacher into a compact feed-forward student across a business-decision simulation on UCI Adult and five OpenML benchmarks: the classification head compresses 53.2M parameters to 8,546 (6,220x); the deployed two-head loan pipeline compresses 111.4M parameters to 17,059 (6,532x). The student retains 95.4-100.5% accuracy and 96.8-100.0% AUC, with the lowest accuracy retention on credit-g at 95.4%; an alpha = 0 hard-label control shows that the teacher's soft targets provide a 2.1-7.0 AUC point gain.

</details>


### [183] [Evaluating Open-Weight E-Commerce Agents with Environment-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16093)
*Nimit Shah,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个确定性、可复现的电商对话评测环境，通过预提交轨迹参数与证据化记录，细粒度评估助手行为，超越单一任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统购物对话评测将多种达成同一购物车的路径压缩为单一任务成功率，掩盖了具体行为缺陷（如过度购买、搜索不佳等）。需要更细粒度、可复现的评测方法。

Method: 构建确定性可复现的电商环境，预提交每次试验的客户与轨迹参数（人设、难度、目标购物车、商品揭示计划）。模拟消费者在模型协助下购买目标商品，环境记录助手动作与状态。基于保留证据对对话各部分分别评估，比如仅当客户提及某商品后才惩罚搜索未展示该商品；并根据与预期工具调用集的比较对不同工具调用施加不同惩罚。环境与模拟器双向交互，可因客户受挫而终止，并实时注入探索、延迟购买、回忆等指令。

Result: 在8个20B至35B参数的开源权重智能体上，每agent 160次试验、44项指标，得到的能力画像可区分行动不足、过度购买、无依据产品属性和糟糕搜索等问题，而这些问题都被终端成功率所掩盖。

Conclusion: 证据化、细粒度的确定性评测环境能揭示终端成功率无法体现的助手行为缺陷，为电商对话系统的能力评估提供更全面可验证的方法。

Abstract: A shopping conversation has many routes to the same cart, and a task-success rate reduces all of them to one score. We build a deterministic and reproducible e-commerce environment that precommits each trial's customer and trajectory parameters, including the persona, difficulty, target cart, and an item reveal schedule. A simulated consumer attempts to buy a target cart from the environment with assistance from the evaluated model. The environment guides the simulator's actions and records every assistant action alongside the environment state at that point. After the trial, these records allow the evaluator to assess individual parts of the conversation against the retained evidence. For example, the evaluator penalizes a search for failing to surface a target product only when the customer has already mentioned that product. We further use this evidence to apply different penalties to tool calls depending on how the assistant's actions compare with an expected tool-call set. Our environment also interacts with the simulator bidirectionally, reading its output to stop the trial when the simulator determines that the customer has become too frustrated and injecting directives in real time that specify when to explore, defer buying an item, or recall a previous exchange. This interaction creates an open-ended and verifiable simulation. Across eight open-weight agents from 20B to 35B parameters, with 160 trials per agent and 44 metrics, the resulting capability profiles distinguish under-action, over-purchase, unsupported product attributes, and poor search, all of which terminal success obscures.

</details>


### [184] [SWB-DM: A Calibrated Sliced-Wasserstein-Barycenter Aggregator with Delayed-Momentum Caching for Byzantine-Robust Federated Learning under Partial Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16099)
*Saranraj S,Saranya M S,Alex David S,Ajay Kumar A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对联邦学习鲁棒聚合中"每轮参与者是全体的公平采样"这一脆弱假设，提出SWB-DM方法：用截断Wasserstein重心(SWB)加基于medoid的规范固定来聚合客户端更新，再用DeMoA式延迟动量跨全体客户端缓存更新，从而将鲁棒性与每轮采样解耦。在448组CIFAR-10及CIFAR-100、FEMNIST和500客户端实验中发现多种机制性失败模式，并揭示IPM攻击比ALIE更能击破顺序统计类防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒聚合方法（coordinate-wise median、Krum、Bulyan、trimmed mean）的有限样本保证依赖于"每轮出现的客户端是全体总体的公平采样"这一假设。但实际上每轮只有少量客户端参与，即使只有适度比例的恶意客户端也能主导该样本，从而悄然使这些保证失效。

Method: 提出SWB-DM：1) SWB将每个客户端更新的每个切片视为一维分布，跨客户端计算截断Wasserstein重心，并通过基于medoid的规范固定步骤恢复坐标身份（作者明确称这是启发式而非标准最优传输理论）；2) 采用DeMoA式延迟动量，每轮跨全体客户端缓存更新，使鲁棒性不依赖于当轮被采样的客户端；3) 截断比例校准很关键，欠截断会在正确校准模型能存活的污染水平下崩溃。

Result: 在448组CIFAR-10配置及CIFAR-100、FEMNIST和500客户端可扩展性实验中发现若干机制性不同的失败模式：等样本下coordinate-wise median退化为确定性错误答案；Krum静默违反自身n>2f+2前提并无预警地发散；Bulyan的n≥4f+3阈值产生尖锐的通过/失败边界。攻击方面，IPM比ALIE更可靠地击败顺序统计类防御（包括SWB），并通过delta空间测量对照收敛界得到证实。SWB-DM的缓存有真实的预热成本，但将所有基线扩展到相同轮次预算后，其CIFAR-10增益异常大；在CIFAR-100上FLTrust受益更多，原因与缓存完全无关。

Conclusion: 鲁棒聚合方法的有效性严重依赖被忽视的采样假设，少量恶意客户端即可在低参与率下破坏其理论保证。SWB-DM通过截断Wasserstein重心加延迟动量缓存将鲁棒性与采样解耦，带来显著增益，但需付出预热成本；同时顺序统计类防御（含SWB）对IPM攻击仍显脆弱，FLTrust等不同机制的方法在特定数据集上表现各异。

Abstract: Robust aggregation methods for federated learning quietly rest on a fragile assumption: that whoever shows up in a given round is a fair sample of the full population. In practice, they rarely are. When only a handful of clients participate per round, even a modest fraction of adversaries can dominate that sample and silently invalidate the finite-sample guarantees that coordinate-wise median, Krum, Bulyan, and trimmed mean all depend on.
  We introduce SWB-DM to address this directly. SWB treats each slice of a client update as a one-dimensional distribution, computes a trimmed Wasserstein barycenter across clients, and recovers coordinate identity via a medoid-based gauge-fixing step -- a heuristic we developed and do not claim it belongs to standard optimal-transport theory. DeMoA-style delayed momentum then caches updates across the full client population each round, decoupling robustness from whoever happened to be sampled. Trim ratio calibration is not cosmetic: under-trimming causes collapse at corruption levels a properly calibrated model survives.
  Across 448 CIFAR-10 configurations, plus CIFAR-100, FEMNIST, and a 500-client scalability run, we find several mechanistically distinct failure modes. Even-sample coordinate-wise median degrades to a deterministic wrong answer. Krum silently violates its own n greater than 2f+2 precondition and diverges without warning. Bulyan's n greater than or equal to 4f+3 threshold produces a sharp pass/fail boundary. On attacks, IPM defeats order-statistic defenses -- including SWB -- more reliably than ALIE, confirmed through delta-space measurements against a convergence bound.
  SWB-DM's cache carries a real warm-up cost, but extending all baselines to the same round budget shows its CIFAR-10 gains are disproportionately large. On CIFAR-100, FLTrust benefits more -- for reasons entirely unrelated to caching.

</details>


### [185] [A Decision-Support Audit Protocol for Supervision Drift in Proxy-Labeled Credit-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16102)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Muhammad Shabanpour,Waldemar Karwowski,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一套锁定式的多信号审计协议，用于区分代理标签信用风险预测中的监督漂移类型（基率漂移、概率尺度漂移、特征-标签关系变化），并在LendingClub数据上做了时间与跨群体迁移验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用风险模型基于代理标签训练，部署时会遇到时间与人群变化，但现有单一迁移指标无法分离基率漂移、概率尺度漂移和特征-标签关系变化，缺乏系统诊断工具。

Method: 设计科学工件：五层锁定式审计协议——迁移性能、oracle-gap探针、校准诊断、特征-标签稳定性、合成阳性对照；阈值与决策规则在解释前锁定。

Result: 在公开LendingClub数据集（2013–2016时间迁移及跨群体迁移）上，排序稳定、oracle gap小；最明显的时间信号是患病率与概率尺度失配，仅截距诊断重校准即可大幅缓解，但原因无法从现有数据版本识别；阳性对照只对较大注入漂移有响应，细微漂移无法排除。

Conclusion: 该协议提供有界解读，将诊断模式映射到治理行动仅为概念性指导，尚未在本研究中验证。

Abstract: Credit-risk models are trained on proxy labels and deployed under temporal and segment change, yet no single transfer metric separates base-rate shift, probability-scale shift, and feature-label relationship change. We contribute a design-science artifact: a locked, multi-signal audit protocol for supervision drift in proxy-labeled credit-risk prediction. Five layers (transfer performance, an oracle-gap probe, a calibration diagnostic, feature-label stability, and a synthetic positive control), thresholds, and decision rules were locked before interpretation; a bounded reading is a designed outcome. On a public LendingClub dataset (temporal 2013 to 2016 and cross-segment transfer), ranking is stable and oracle gaps are small; the clearest temporal signal is a prevalence and probability-scale mismatch that intercept-only diagnostic recalibration largely reduces, though its cause is not identifiable from the available release. The positive control responds only to larger injected shifts; subtler drift cannot be excluded. Mapping diagnostic patterns to governance actions is conceptual guidance, not validated here.

</details>


### [186] [LLMs as Master Forgers: Generating Synthetic Time Series Data for Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16155)
*Mantek Singh,Jeshwanth Challagundla,Prateek Karnal,Gagan Ganapathy,Vineet Shah,Ridam Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用LLM生成制造业时序合成数据的框架，通过微调与RAG增强数据多样性，性能优于ARIMA和LSTM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实制造场景中带标签时序数据稀缺，制约了稳健机器学习模型的开发，因此需要生成高质量合成时序数据来弥补数据不足。

Method: 在制造工艺指令上微调预训练LLM，并引入检索增强生成（RAG）提升合成数据的多样性与真实性；与ARIMA、LSTM等传统时序建模方法对比。

Result: 在定量指标、PCA分析和下游任务（异常检测）表现上，LLM驱动框架均优于基线，能有效捕捉真实制造数据的时序依赖与统计特性，并提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: LLM结合微调与RAG可生成高质量制造业合成时序数据，有效缓解数据稀缺问题并提升下游任务表现，为制造领域时序建模提供新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate synthetic time series data for manufacturing processes. Motivated by the scarcity of labeled time-series data in real-world manufacturing settings, which hinders the development of robust machine learning models, we explore the potential of LLMs to learn complex temporal dependencies and generate realistic synthetic data. Our approach involves fine-tuning pre-trained LLMs on manufacturing process instructions and employing a Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) technique to enhance data diversity and realism. We evaluate our method against traditional time series modeling techniques like ARIMA and LSTMs, using quantitative metrics, PCA analysis, and downstream task performance (anomaly detection).
